                                                                    Chairman’s Statement


本人欣然向各位股東提呈龍湖集團控股有限公司        I am pleased to present to our shareholders the half-year business review and
（「本公司」，連同其附屬公司，統稱「本集團」）截     outlook for the six months ended June 30, 2024 of Longfor Group Holdings
至二零二四年六月三十日止之半年業務回顧與展        Limited (the “Company”, and together with its subsidiaries, collectively the
望。                           “Group”).

過去的半年，宏觀經濟延續調整態勢。房地產行        Over the past six months, the overall macroeconomic condition continued to be
業逐步告別過去的「高負債、高周轉、高槓桿」的       highly dynamic with rapid adjustments. The real estate industry has gradually
「三高」模式，多輪政策持續發力，以更好的支持       transitioned away from the previous “three highs” model, characterized by
剛性和改善性住房需求，加快構建房地產發展新        high indebtedness, high turnover, and high leverage. In response to such
模式。                          circumstances, multiple policies have been implemented to better support
                             both rigid and upgrade demand for housing, with a goal to accelerate the
                             establishment of a new industry development model.

龍湖集團圍繞經營性現金流、提效增利、穩步壓        Longfor Group has implemented and firmly upheld our core objectives including
降負債及優化存貨結構等核心目標，堅定執行、        enhancing operating cash flow, improving efficiency and increasing profitability,
積極應對。本集團上半年核心權益後淨利實現人        steadily reducing indebtedness and optimizing inventory structure. For the
民幣47.5億元，其中，運營及服務業務收入實現      first half of the year, the Group reported a core attributable profit of RMB4.75
人民幣131億元，同比增長7.6%，利潤佔比進一     billion, with revenues from investment property operation and services and others
步提升至八成以上，助力本集團整體利潤的穩定        reaching RMB13.1 billion, representing a year-on-year increase of 7.6%, the
和結構的優化。                      corresponding profit contribution further increased to more than 80% and has
                             contributed to stabilizing our overall profit and optimizing our structure.

本集團始終將財務穩健放在首位，短期債務的提        The Group prioritizes sustaining a solid financial position, with an emphasis
前削減優先於增量投資。截至年中，本集團有息        on early repayment of short-term debt over incremental investments. As of
負債人民幣1,874億元，較年初減少人民幣52億     mid-year, our interest-bearing debt amounted to RMB187.4 billion, falling
元。平均融資成本為4.16%，平均合同借貸年期      by RMB5.2 billion since the beginning of the year. Our average finance cost
延長至9.19年。有息負債的80%以上來自於銀行     stood at 4.16%, and the average contract borrowing period has extended to 9.19
支持，安全的財務盤面將持續穩固我們與戰略銀        years. Importantly, over 80% of our interest-bearing debt is supported by bank
行之間的合作，並堅定我們穿越週期的信心。未        financing, establishing a secure financial position that continues to solidify our
來，我們將繼續提前鋪排有息負債的壓降，確保        partnerships with strategic banks and reinforces our confidence in navigating
本公司財務盤面持續安全穩健。               economic cycles. Going forward, we will continue to plan for the reduction of
                             interest bearing debt in advance to ensure the continued safety and stability of the
                             Company’s financial situation.

2024年1-6月全國商品房銷售額實現人民幣4.71   The national new home sales from January to June 2024 totaled RMB4.71
萬億元，同比跌幅25%，新房銷售表現在宏觀經       trillion, representing a year-on-year decline of 25%, continued to face significant
濟緩慢恢復下依然有壓力。本集團開發業態以銷        pressure amid a stagnant economic recovery. The Group’s development strategy
定支、謹慎佈局，上半年在北京、上海、蘇州         focuses on prudent land acquisition and sales. In the first half of the year, we
等高能級城市獲取7塊新地；同時，注重彈性供        acquired seven new plots in high-tier cities such as Beijing, Shanghai, and
貨、精細化管理，存貨積極出清、新貨提速去         Suzhou. At the same time, we have remained flexible in terms of saleable
化，以驅動現金流、回款及利潤的達成。           resources supply and have enhanced precision in management, actively reduced

     主席報告
     Chairman’s Statement


     截至6月末，龍湖商業已在20個重點城市開業91                              By the end of June, Longfor Commercial has opened 91 shopping malls in 20
     座商場，整體出租率達96%。近兩年，居民生活                               core cities, achieving an overall occupancy rate of 96%. Over the past two years,
     方式和觀念持續變化，消費支出的重點正在轉向                                continued shifts in residents’ lifestyles and perspectives have led to a growing
     舒心服務和場景體驗。龍湖商業運營團隊緊跟市                                consumption expenditure emphasis on wellbeing services and immersive
     場趨勢，通過項目改造升級及高效運營，積極調                                experience. Longfor Commercial operations team has adeptly responded to these
     整業態結構與品類，增強消費粘性，帶動商戶經                                market trends by actively adjusting our product mix through project renovations
     營提升，從而持續創造租金收入及利潤的增長貢                                and efficient operation, thereby enhancing consumer engagement, improving
     獻。                                                   tenant operations, and generating continued growth in rental income and profits.

     龍湖集團長租公寓品牌「冠寓」從租戶需求出發，                               Longfor Group’s long-term rental apartment brand, Goyoo, has made significant

     房產去化及增量住房優化，繼續支持剛性和改善                                toward phasing out existing real estate stock and optimizing new housing
     性住房需求，加快構建房地產發展新模式，推動                                offerings since the beginning of the year. The focus remains on supporting both
     行業的長遠和可持續發展。                                         rigid and upgrade demand for housing, expediting the evolution of a new real
                                                          estate development model, and fostering sustainable and long-term industry
                                                          development.

     資、資產管理、物業管理、智慧營造五大航道業                                and services and others and firmly pursue the synergies between five main

     不斷迭代與升級組織系統，強化專業能力、提升                                management, property management, and smart construction. By maintaining
     運營效率。                                                a customer-centric approach, the Group continuously refines and updates its

面對未來，本集團將繼續圍繞高質量發展的核心      Looking ahead, the Group will remain steadfast in its core strategy of high-
戰略，堅持穩健的財務管理策略，有序壓降債務      quality development. Longfor will adhere to a prudent financial management
規模，持續調整存貨結構，優化利潤結構，以實      strategy, orderly reduce debt scale, continuously adjust inventory structures, and
現穩健、可持續的發展。                optimize profit structure to ensure stable and sustainable growth.

限制性股份激勵計劃                           RESTRICTED SHARE AWARD SCHEME
本公司已經採用限制性股份激勵計劃，計劃目的               To provide long-term incentive for employees, assist to retain its existing
為僱員提供長期激勵，協助本集團保留其現有僱               employees and attract more talents, and provide human resources protection
員及吸引更多優秀人才，為本集團的長期戰略目               for the long-term strategic goals of the Group, the Company has adopted the
標提供人力資源保障。根據此計劃，受託人將以               Restricted Share Award Scheme. According to the scheme, trustees will purchase
本公司提供的現金及信托自有資源（累計已購買               shares from the market out of cash contributed by the Company and the trust’s
未歸屬或未授予股票收到的分紅）於市場上購買               own resources (the dividend accumulated from the shares purchased but not yet
現有股份，並以信託形式代相關選定僱員持有，               vested or granted) which shall be held on trust for the relevant selected employees
直至該等股份按計劃規則歸屬予相關選定僱員為               until such shares are vested with the relevant selected employees in accordance
止。                                  with the rules of the scheme.

     企業管治                                                 CORPORATE GOVERNANCE
     本公司知悉公司透明度及問責十分重要，致力於                                The Company recognises the importance of corporate transparency and
     達致高水準的企業管治及通過更有效的企業管治                                accountability. We are committed in achieving a high standard of corporate
     帶領本集團取得更好業績及提升公司價值。於截                                governance and leading the Group to attain better results and enhance company
     至二零二四年六月三十日止六個月，本公司一直                                value with effective corporate governance procedures. During the six months
     採用、應用及遵守上市規則附錄C1第二部分所                                ended June 30, 2024, the Company has adopted, applied and complied with
     載的企業管治守則（「守則」）所載守則條文。惟以                              the code provisions as set out in the Corporate Governance Code (the “Code”)
     下偏離除外：                                               contained in Part 2 of Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules, except with the
                                                          following deviation:

     於二零二二年十月二十八日委任陳序平先生為董                                Following the appointment of Mr. Chen Xuping as the Chairman of the Board
     事會主席生效後，陳序平先生身兼本公司董事                                 with effect from October 28, 2022, Mr. Chen Xuping assumes the dual roles of
     會主席及首席執行官兩職。是項委任與守則第                                 the Chairman of the Board and the Chief Executive Officer of the Company. This
     C.2.1守則條文有所偏離，其規定主席與行政總                              deviates from code provision C.2.1 of the Code, which requires that the roles
     裁之角色應有區分，並不應由一人同時兼任。於                                of chairman and the chief executive officer should be separate and should not
     評估本集團之發展情況並考慮陳序平先生之經                                 be performed by the same individual. After evaluating the development of the
     驗，董事會認為本公司董事會主席及首席執行官                                Group and taking into account of the experience of Mr. Chen Xuping, the Board
     由一人同時兼任於現階段符合本公司之最佳利                                 was of the opinion that it is in the best interest of the Company at the present
     益，有利於執行本集團之發展戰略。惟董事會將                                stage for vesting the roles of the Chairman of the Board and the Chief Executive
     不時檢討此架構並將於適當時候考慮將兩個職位                                Officer of the Company in the same person as it helps to facilitate the execution
     分開。                                                  of the Group’s development strategies. The Board will nevertheless review this
                                                          structure from time to time and will consider the segregation of the two roles at
                                                          the appropriate time.

     審閱範圍                                                 SCOPE OF REVIEW
     吾等已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審閱委聘                                We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong Standard on Review
     準則第2410號「由實體的獨立核數師執行中期財                              Engagements 2410 “Review of Interim Financial Information Performed by
     務資料審閱」進行審閱。審閱該等簡明綜合財務                                the Independent Auditor of the Entity” issued by the Hong Kong Institute of
     報表的工作包括主要向負責財務與會計事務的人                                Certified Public Accountants. A review of these condensed consolidated financial
     員作出查詢，進行分析以及其他審閱程序。審閱                                statements consists of making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for
     的範圍遠小於根據香港審計準則進行審核的範                                 financial and accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review
     圍，因此無法確保吾等可知悉在審核中可能發現                                procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit conducted in
     的所有重大事項，故吾等並無發表審核意見。                                 accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing and consequently does not
                                                          enable us to obtain assurance that we would become aware of all significant
                                                          matters that might be identified in an audit. Accordingly, we do not express an
                                                          audit opinion.

     結論                                                   CONCLUSION
     基於吾等的審閱，吾等並無發現令吾等相信簡明                                Based on our review, nothing has come to our attention that causes us to believe
     綜合財務報表在任何重大方面未有根據國際會計                                that the condensed consolidated financial statements are not prepared, in all
     準則第34號編製的任何事項。                                       material respects, in accordance with IAS 34.

3.   分部資料                    3.   SEGMENT INFORMATION
     本集團根據主要經營決策人（「主要經營決          The Group determines its operating segments based on internal reports
     策人」）
        （ 即本公司執行董事）為向分部配          about components of the Group that are regularly reviewed by the chief
     置資源及評估其表現而定期檢討的有關本           operating decision maker (“CODM”) (i.e., the executive directors of the
     集團組成部分的內部報告，釐定其經營分           Company) in order to allocate resources to the segment and to assess its
     部。                           performance.

     本集團按活動類別組成業務單元，並據此           The Group is organised into business units based on their types of
     編製資料而向本集團主要經營決策人呈報           activities, based on which information is prepared and reported to the
     以便配置資源及評估表現。本集團根據國           Group’s CODM for the purposes of resource allocation and assessment of
     際財務報告準則第8號經營分部 釐定的經          performance. The Group’s operating segments under IFRS 8 Operating
     營分部可分為以下三項主要業務：              Segments are identified as the following three main operations:

     •    開發業務：該分部指發展及銷售辦         •     Property development: this segment represents the development
          公樓、商業及住宅物業。本集團這               and sales of office premises, commercial and residential properties.
          方面的業務在中國開展。                   The Group’s activities in this regard are carried out in the PRC.

     •    運營業務：該分部指租賃本集團自         •     Investment property operation: this segment represents the lease of
          主發展或轉租的投資物業，以賺取               investment properties, which are self-developed or under subleases
          租金收入並長期從物業增值中獲取               by the Group to generate rental income and to gain from the
          收益。本集團目前的投資物業組合               appreciation in the properties’ values in the long term. Currently,
          主要包括購物商場及租賃住房，全               the Group’s investment property portfolio mainly comprises
          部位於中國。                        shopping malls and rental housing and are all located in the PRC.

     •    服務業務及其他：該分部主要透過         •     Services and others: this segment mainly represents the income

           分部盈利會就未具體劃撥至個別分                segments, such as directors’ and auditor’s remuneration and other

  級。已落成投資物業的公平值乃按年期收             periods. The fair value of completed investment properties are determined
  益率、可能重訂租約收益率、貼現率、每             by term yield, reversionary yield, discount rate, monthly term rental
  月定期租金及可能重訂租金釐定。發展中             and reversionary rental. The fair values of investment properties under
  投資物業的公平值乃按總發展價值、開發             development are determined by gross development value, developer’s
  商的利潤及貼現率釐定。                    profit and discount rate.

  計及未付利息（如有）及(2)提前贖回金額。        redemption and (2) the Make Whole Price.

  （包括(1)於發行日期最初發行的票據及(2)       10% of the aggregate principal amount (1) originally issued on issue date

  期發行的任何額外票據）仍未贖回的當日           “Further Issues” remain outstanding, the Company may at its option

  回日期的以下項目之和：(1)相關各項優先         redemption date, the sum of (1) the present value of the principal amount

  於報告期末，本集團有指定為高效對沖工         At the end of the reporting period, the Group had cross currency interest
  具的交叉貨幣利率掉期，以透過將一定比         rate swaps designated as highly effective hedging instruments in order
  例的定息美元優先票據及相應利息付款由         to minimise its exposures to foreign currency and cash flow interest
  美元轉換為人民幣來盡量減低本集團承受         rate risk on its fixed rate USD senior notes and corresponding interest
  的該等借款及優先票據及相應利息付款的         payment by swapping a portion of those borrowings and senior notes and
  外幣及現金流量利率風險。               corresponding interest payment from USD to RMB.

  於報告期末，本集團有指定為有效對沖工         At the end of reporting period, the Group had foreign currency forward
  具的外匯遠期合約及封頂封底遠期合約，         contracts and CFF contracts designated as effective hedging instruments in
  以通過於借款到期日以人民幣兌換港元來         order to minimise foreign currency risk on its HKD bank borrowings by
  盡量減低其港元銀行借貸的外匯風險。          selling RMB in exchange for HKD at the borrowings maturity dates.

  交叉貨幣利率掉期、利率掉期、外匯遠期         The terms of the cross currency interest rate swaps, interest rate swaps,
  合約及封頂封底遠期合約的條款經已磋商         foreign currency forward contracts and CFF contracts have been negotiated
  以配合各項指定對沖項目的條款，而董事         to match the terms of the respective designated hedged items and the
  認為該等交叉貨幣利率掉期、利率掉期、         directors consider that the cross currency interest rate swaps, interest rate
  外匯遠期合約及封頂封底遠期合約屬有效         swaps, foreign currency forward contracts and CFF contracts are effective
  對沖工具。                      hedging instruments.

  於二零二四年六月三十日，本集團就本集              The Group provided guarantees amounting to RMB15,855,310,000 (at

  幣15,855,310,000元（於二零二三年十二月      of mortgage bank loans granted to purchasers of the Group’s properties.
  三十一日：人民幣19,092,799,000元）的擔      Guarantees are given to banks with respect to loans procured by the
  保。本集團就本集團物業買方之銀行貸款              purchasers of the Group’s properties. Such guarantees will be released by
  向銀行提供擔保。該等擔保將於向買方交              banks upon delivery of the properties to the purchasers and completion of
  收物業及相關按揭物業登記完成時由銀行              the relevant mortgage properties registration.
  解除。


  本公司董事認為，本集團物業買方的該等              In the opinion of the directors of the Company, the fair values of these
  財務擔保合約的公平值於初始確認時並不              financial guarantee contracts to the purchasers of the Group’s properties
  重大，以及本公司董事認為向客戶提供的              are insignificant at initial recognition and the directors of the Company
  財務擔保的信貸風險極低，原因為買方之              consider that the credit risk exposure to financial guarantees provided
  過往可觀察違約比率較低及融資以物業作              to customers are remote because the historical observed default ratio by
  抵押且物業的市價高於擔保金額，因此，              purchasers is relatively low and the facilities are secured by the properties
  於二零二四年六月三十日及二零二三年               and the market price of the properties is higher than the guaranteed
  十二月三十一日的報告期末因擔保合約產              amounts, accordingly, the expected credit loss arising from the guarantee

25. 以股份為基礎的付款交易                25. SHARE-BASED PAYMENT TRANSACTIONS
  本公司的限制性股份激勵計劃（「限制性股             The Company’s restricted share award scheme (the “Restricted Share
  份激勵計劃」）及購股權計劃（「購股權計             Award Scheme”) and share option scheme (the “Share Option Scheme”)
  劃」）乃分別根據於二零一四年十月二十八             were adopted pursuant to resolutions passed on October 28, 2014 and
  日及二零零九年十二月二十三日通過的決              December 23, 2009 respectively. The objective of the Restricted Share
  議案獲採納。限制性股份激勵計劃的目的              Award Scheme is to recognise and motivate the contribution of directors
  在於認可及激勵本集團董事及僱員作出的              and employees of the Group, to provide incentives, to help the Group
  貢獻，提供激勵，幫助本集團挽留其現有              in retaining its existing employees and recruiting additional employees
  僱員及招募額外僱員，並為彼等提供經濟              and to provide them with economic interests in attaining the long-term
  利益，以實現本集團的長期業務目標。購              business objectives of the Group. The primary purpose of Share Option
  股權計劃的主要目的在於向董事及合資格              Scheme is to provide incentives to directors and eligible employees. The
  僱員提供激勵。股份激勵計劃容許本集團              share award scheme allows the Group to make bonus payments to eligible
  向合資格人士派發由一名獨立受託人購入              persons by way of the Company’s shares acquired by and held through
  及持有（直至指定的歸屬條件達成為止）的             an independent trustee until fulfillment of specified conditions before
  本公司股份作為花紅。                      vesting.

27. 金融工具的公平值計量               27. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS OF FINANCIAL
                                 INSTRUMENTS
  按經常性基準以公平值計量的本                Fair value of the Group’s financial assets and liabilities that are
  集團金融資產及負債公平值                  measured at fair value on a recurring basis
  本集團部分金融資產及負債於各報告期末            Some of the Group’s financial assets and liabilities are measured at fair
  按公平值計量。下表提供如何計量該等             value at the end of each reporting period. The following table gives
  金融資產及負債之公平值（特別是所使用            information about how the fair values of these financial assets and
  之估值技術及輸入數據），及根據公平值            liabilities are determined (in particular, the valuation technique(s) and
  計量輸入數據之可觀測程度所劃分之公平            inputs used), as well as the level of the fair value hierarchy into which the
  值計量之公平值級別（第一至三層級）之資           fair value measurements are categorised (levels 1 to 3) based on the degree
  料。                            to which the inputs to the fair value measurements is observable.

  •    第一層級公平值計量是按活躍市場          •     Level 1 fair value measurements are those derived from quoted
       上相同資產或負債報價（不作調整）               prices (unadjusted) in active market for identical assets or liabilities;
       得出之公平值計量；


  •    第二層級公平值計量是指第一層級          •     Level 2 fair value measurements are those derived from inputs other
       之報價以外，可直接（即價格）或間               than quoted prices included within Level 1 that are observable for
       接（即由價格得出）觀察之資產或負               the asset or liability, either directly (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e.
       債得出之公平值計量；及                    derived from prices); and

  •    第三層級公平值計量是指以市場不          •     Level 3 fair value measurements are those derived from valuation
       可觀察之數據為依據，作資產或負                techniques that include inputs for the asset or liability that are not
       債之相關輸入數據（不可觀察輸入                based on observable market data (unobservable inputs).
       數據），以估值技術得出之公平值
       計量。
